## 2026-10-29 — DMD-on-range-time candidate screened and closed

Applying standard DMD to the cached range-time maps would transfer an existing spatiotemporal feature method into the same gesture task. Sharma & Panda 2024 already use DMD for vision-based hand gestures; Xue et al. 2026 already model temporal dynamics on this exact corpus; radar DMD target-processing prior art exists. No direct exact IR-UWB-DMD classifier hit was found, but that bounded absence does not clear novelty. With only clutter-removed real magnitude maps and no hand-motion truth, the candidate lacks a verified radar-specific mechanism. Closed before data-value/label access or fit. No process/checkpoint is active; restore remains verified and original 789-file source untouched. Method contribution remains unvalidated. See `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261029_dmd_spatiotemporal_gate/DECISION_NOTE.md`.

## 2026-10-29 — directed inter-range information-flow screen

Transfer entropy between adjacent range-bin envelopes was screened as a possible directed motion cue. The bounded literature review found no exact UWB-gesture application, but the core estimator is generic (Schreiber 2000), while multi-range gesture signatures and temporal modeling of range-time maps already have direct radar/same-corpus precedents (Ritchie & Jones 2019; Xue et al. 2026). With 90 frames per clip and no synchronized hand-kinematic truth, estimator choices would be consequential and a score gain would not validate the proposed physical mechanism. Closed before data-value/label read or fitting. No UWB process/checkpoint is active; restored data SHA is already verified; original 789-file source snapshot remains untouched. Method goal remains unvalidated. See `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261029_transfer_entropy_gate/DECISION_NOTE.md`.

# 2026-10-29 UTC — cross-range-bin co-modulation candidate gate

A bounded source search screened neighboring range-bin envelope co-modulation as a phase-free, single-view candidate observable in the clutter-removed maps. No exact IR-UWB descriptor hit was found, but the feature is generic second-order texture/correlation and overlaps established magnitude/TOA descriptors, hand localization, and the same-corpus global/local time-range model. Without kinematic truth, a gain would not verify the proposed physical explanation. Candidate not cleared; no arrays, labels, fitting, training, or parameter search were used. Decision note: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261029_cross_range_coupling_gate/DECISION_NOTE.md`. This is a bounded no-go for this candidate, not a conclusion that all current-data methods are impossible.

# 2026-10-28 UTC — single-view mechanism gate reconciliation

A read-only synthesis of prior literature/engineering gates found no eligible candidate among the already screened local echo matching, range-ridge/energy/area, range-time trajectory, range-resolved recurrence/RQA, temporal-reversal label mapping, and generic multiview/evidence fusion routes. The 2026-10-04 local echo diagnostic passed its joint gate for 0/6 source users in each view and did not evaluate classifier accuracy; this closes only that registered matching configuration. No model was trained, no data values or labels were read, and no parameter grid was run. This synthesis does not conclude all current-data methods are impossible. It records which common routes must not be repackaged and keeps the method-contribution goal unresolved. No new data request is made. # 2026-10-28 11:13 UTC — temporal-reversal label-transform novelty gate closed

A source-only screen evaluated reversing the slow-time order of the current clutter-removed range-time maps and swapping to each gesture’s opposite ordered-motion label. The operation is directly relevant to UWB gesture classes but is not method-novel: Molchanov et al. (CVPR Workshops 2015) already reverse frame order and map left-swipe labels to right-swipe; a wireless-sensing ICASSP 2020 paper also studies gesture time reversal. The proposed UWB adaptation therefore does not clear the method gate. No arrays, label values, or predictions were read; there was no training, fitting, tuning, or download. Full-text retrieval was incomplete, so this is a bounded prior-art screen, not an exhaustive review. Decision note: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261028_temporal_reversal_label_gate/DECISION_NOTE.md`.

The previous archived prediction audit remains the latest completed experiment: 136/136 checks across eight folds and seven fixed heads; it reproduced the saved full-view metric, not a method contribution. No experiment is authorized. The method-contribution goal remains unresolved.

Direct full text for Xue, Yang & Zhu (2026), DOI 10.3389/fnins.2026.1864448, confirms the same UWB-Gestures/XeThru X4 corpus, 12 classes, Left/Top/Right views, 20-fps 90×189 TR maps, 9,600 examples/eight users, and a 6-user/2-user subject-disjoint split. Their ConvMixer local branch + CNN-Transformer global branch and gated first-order/bilinear second-order fusion report 98.29% tri-view accuracy. This preempts generic same-corpus TR, local/global, adaptive/bilinear fusion, and basic multi-view-fusion novelty claims. Its single 6/2 split and 9,600-example count differ from our quality-filtered 9,300 examples and eight held-user folds, so scores are not directly comparable. Eom et al. (2025), DOI 10.1109/JIOT.2025.3556901, also preempt generic UWB open-set recognition via temporal/spatial transformers and one-versus-rest deep inspectors.

The only data available are the existing clutter-removed real-valued 3-view maps—no phase-coherent raw echoes, calibrated view costs, acquisition hardware, or synchronized kinematic truth. No distinct candidate cleared novelty and input-observability gates. The research contribution remains unvalidated; do not relabel the restore, audit replay, generic fusion, or re-used eight-user findings as a new method. # 2026-10-12 — Adaptive radar-view selection prior-art gate

A bounded primary-source review screened per-instance selection among the three X4 views to trade recognition loss against view/sensor cost. Generic instance-wise sparse sensor selection jointly minimizing HAR loss and number of sensors is already established by Yang et al., AAAI 2020, DOI 10.1609/aaai.v34i01.5461 (MDP policy, imitation learning, Mutual DAgger). The same 12-class UWB-Gestures corpus also has a 2026 three-view IR-UWB fusion paper claiming adaptive feature selection (Xue et al., DOI 10.3389/fnins.2026.1864448; reported split comparability remains unverified). A two-radar angular-diversity gesture paper uses sparse time-frequency features and fusion for poor-view robustness (Yang et al., DOI 10.1049/iet-rsn.2018.5206). Thus generic cost-aware view selection/adaptive multiview fusion is not cleared as a standalone contribution.

No experiment was run. Current cloud has no radar acquisition hardware or calibrated measured per-view cost. Future work would require a signal-specific mechanism beyond these priors and a valid measured cost target; do not fit a generic selector or rebrand fusion. Full report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261012_adaptive_view_selection_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`.

# 2026-10-11 — FMCW cohort's public detection baseline reviewed

The public `uctopus/hand_gesture_recognition` implementation, paired with the Zenodo/IEEE FMCW corpus, computes range/Doppler/angle/magnitude features, anchors a 20-frame gesture window at a thresholded minimum-range frame, creates background windows, and uses a two-layer LSTM for five gesture classes plus background. This is implementation-level prior art for peak-anchored window localization and lightweight temporal classification on FMCW. The repository's reported metrics are self-reported, not independently reproduced, and its engineered input differs from X4's restored IR-UWB range-time maps. A project method based only on a min-range anchor, fixed window, background examples, or generic LSTM is not cleared. Sources and scope are appended to `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261011_zenodo_framelabel_cohort_gate/RUN_REPORT.md`. No dataset was downloaded and no project fitting ran.

# 2026-10-11 research update — paired-event contrastive candidate not cleared

The candidate of sample-level same-event cross-view consistency is not a method contribution as currently specified. Primary-source precedents include ViewCon (Shah et al., WACV 2023), which learns viewpoint-invariant multi-view action representations and reports domain-generalization benefits, and DVANet (Siddiqui et al., AAAI 2024), which explicitly separates action/view features using supervised contrastive losses. The X4 same-corpus MV-GLFNet paper already covers the single/dual/tri-view fusion family. A held-physical-view test split can be retained as a diagnostic, but a split plus generic contrastive objective does not establish novelty. See `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261011_cross_view_contrastive_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md` for scope, citations, and executor audit.

No labels, tensors, or checkpoint contents were read; no model fit or training started. Novel, effective contribution remains unvalidated.

## 2026-10-10 — Author-code clarification for multiview domain-generalization prior art

The authors' public MVRADAR implementation encodes RT/DT/RDT/ERT/ART representations from one TI MIMO mmWave radar; it provides feature fusion, optional domain-adversarial learning, and Dempster–Shafer evidence fusion. This confirms broad prior art for multirepresentation radar DG/fusion, but is distinct from three independent X4 viewpoint sensors and does not establish leave-one-physical-view-out evaluation. That evaluation protocol remains a benchmark gap only; no original method is identified. No project labels/arrays were read and no fitting/training ran. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_view_generalization_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-10 — Held-view generalization prior-art gate

The 2026 same-corpus MV-GLFNet paper tests individual left/right/top views and all dual/tri-view input combinations under a 6-subject train / 2-subject test split. It does not report training on two views and testing on a held-out third view. Yang et al. 2018 establishes dual-radar angular diversity and robustness as prior art. Leave-one-view-out evaluation may be a testable benchmark question, but is not itself an original method. A related 2025 mmWave multiview domain-generalization paper (DOI 10.1109/TIM.2025.3637962) was found only through bibliographic/abstract metadata; publisher full text remains a required review and was not used to clear novelty.

A possible outer split is held-subject plus held-view across the eight subjects and three fixed views (24 cells), with the target view excluded from all fitting and selection. Before running, view-to-view sample correspondence must be confirmed by provenance metadata. The available clutter-removed maps do not provide synchronized motion ground truth or radar calibration, so no arbitrary-position, physical-invariance, or calibrated trajectory claim is supported. No data labels/array values were read and no model was fit/trained. No candidate method cleared novelty; do not begin this grid. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_view_generalization_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-10 — Recurrence / RQA prior-art gate

A bounded screen found RQA/Poincaré features already used for CW-radar micro-Doppler human-action classification (Rani et al., IEEE Sensors Journal 2021, DOI 10.1109/JSEN.2021.3126436; abstract/metadata only) and RQA for general human-activity recognition (Ngo et al., EMBC 2020, DOI 10.1109/EMBC44109.2020.9176347). Generic recurrence-plot/RQA radar gesture processing is not a cleared novelty claim. The exact use of range-resolved recurrence on this IR-UWB dataset remains unreviewed and may be testable, but is only an application candidate until method-level distinction is established. No labels/arrays were read; no training or fitting occurred. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_recurrence_gate/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-10 — Temporal-reversal augmentation prior-art gate

Generic temporal sequence inversion/reversal as radar gesture augmentation is directly present in RaGeoSense (Scientific Reports 2025, DOI 10.1038/s41598-025-00065-8); Retro-Actions (ICCVW 2019) provides action-recognition prior art for time reversal with class-label transforms. We therefore closed generic reverse-sequence augmentation as a standalone novelty claim. Public UWB-Gestures class names contain inverse swipe pairs and CW/CCW, but inward-push lacks an outward-pull counterpart, so the full label set is not closed under this action. Current clutter-removed 90-row inputs and acquisition metadata provide no frame-level motion truth to validate a reversed sample as a physical reverse action. No labels/arrays were read, no fit/train ran, and no active training was found. This gate does not rule out all specific equivariant models, which would need separate review. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_time_reversal_gate/RUN_REPORT.md`.



## 2026-10-10 — Restored raw-echo availability gate

Read only the restored dataset file inventory and sizes. The model input is `cache/X_clutterremoved.npy`; the restored directory has no raw echo matrix, per-radar empty/background capture, source/antenna transfer calibration, or synchronization record. Do not read labels/array values to infer these. This closes alternate raw-echo clutter-filter comparisons and physical de-embedding on this restored subset; it does not assert no separate archive could ever contain raw data. Published UWB-Gestures preprocessing already documents loopback clutter filtering, while 2026 same-corpus MV-GLFNet uses MTI and notes the weak-temporal-gesture tradeoff. A generic filter swap is neither novelty-cleared nor testable from the current cache. No labels/arrays were opened; no fitting or training occurred. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_raw_signal_availability/RUN_REPORT.md`. Continue only with questions both distinct from reviewed prior art and testable on the existing clutter-removed inputs; otherwise await calibrated multi-hardware data and synchronized motion truth.


## 2026-10-10 — Few-shot user adaptation and motion-feature prior art

A bounded primary-literature screen found a July 2026 IEEE IoT Journal paper, Tian et al., “Latent Space Consistency Learning for Few-Shot Individual Gesture Recognition With Millimeter-Wave Radar,” DOI 10.1109/JIOT.2026.3687630. Its abstract describes user-conditioned dual-prototype consistency, source-free few-label target-user adaptation with VAE latent/z-space alignment, KD, and L2-SP. This preempts generic few-shot user personalization/prototype-consistency claims as standalone novelty. The target sensor family is mmWave; the review did not establish protocol equivalence to the project's X4 IR-UWB eight held-user folds and did not obtain full text. Existing micro-Doppler/time-frequency radar gesture papers and IR-UWB direction/hand-area feature papers also preempt generic Doppler/time-frequency feature claims. This round read no project labels, arrays, or checkpoints and ran no fit/training.

Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261010_fewshot_gesture_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`. No candidate cleared the novelty gate; do not start an adaptation or feature grid. Continue only with a materially distinct, primary-literature-reviewed mechanism feasible on the existing data, or wait for the missing independent calibrated radar hardware and synchronized motion truth. Details and sources are recorded in the report.


# 2026-10-09 — Compact and speed-robust UWB prior-art gate

A bounded review found direct prior art for generic lightweight UWB gesture models (Qiao et al., IEEE JSTARS 2023, DOI 10.1109/JSTARS.2023.3274830), same-corpus CNN-LSTM/attention feature fusion and synthetic-noise robustness (Khan & Kwon, IEEE Access 2025, DOI 10.1109/ACCESS.2025.3558293; abstract only), and same-corpus speed-induced multi-scale features (Kim et al., IEEE Access 2026, DOI 10.1109/ACCESS.2026.3671243). The reviewed papers do not establish protocol equivalence with the project's eight held-user folds, so their headline accuracies are not direct comparisons. Generic compact-CNN, feature-fusion, noise-robustness, and speed/multi-scale claims are not cleared as standalone novelties. No labels/arrays/checkpoints were read and no fitting/training was run. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261009_compact_speed_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-09 — Multi-view synchronization evidence review

The UWB-Gestures descriptor says the three X4 radars operate independently and stores views separately; each sample is 90 slow-time rows (4.5 s). The descriptor does not describe a shared trigger, timestamps, or synchronization/calibration procedure. This omission is not proof that data were captured asynchronously. The 2026 MV-GLFNet paper assumes aligned TR dimensions after identical preprocessing and notes view-alignment and possible synchronization/calibration costs in deployment. Accordingly, sample-index equality is not evidence for physical phase/time alignment; coherent phase fusion, propagation-delay estimation, and calibrated 3-D trajectory claims are unsupported by current public acquisition metadata.

Same-corpus single/dual/tri-view fusion is already published, and generic radar-camera DTW alignment is adjacent prior art. This review does not clear a new alignment method. No labels/arrays were read; no training started. The restored 86-file transfer receipt remains verified, and process inspection found no active run. Detailed source and gate: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261009_multiview_sync_review/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-09 Pantomime rights and movement-semantics follow-up

The Aalto institutional catalog lists the Pantomime dataset as CC-BY-4.0 and its access link targets Zenodo record 4459969. The Zenodo page is Open and lists both data and source archives, but its visible license field is blank; the catalog-to-archive association and member manifest are not independently verified. This supports dataset reuse at the catalog level, not a blanket license for the separate source archive.

The public class descriptions yield only name-level directional-swipe/circular-motion overlap with UWB-Gestures. No matched kinematic instructions, handedness, axis, amplitude, or timing evidence was established. Generic heterogeneous radar gesture transfer is prior art; a bounded search not finding the exact endpoint is not novelty clearance. Thus this remains a blocked candidate, not a method or contribution.

Executor check: Zenodo is not allowlisted; no data archive was fetched, no local labels/arrays were read, and no model was trained. Existing UWB data and the 789-file source snapshot remain available. No manual upload is needed. Resume only after a distinct sensor-physics question, independent movement mapping, verified manifest/rights association, and frozen participant-disjoint protocol pass. Detailed record: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261009_pantomime_rights_semantics/RUN_REPORT.md`.

No archive was downloaded or extracted, and dataset rights do not imply source-code rights.

## 2026-10-08 在线手势起始检测不可由当前分段包验证

定向阅读全文 Seifi 等 2025 年 MIRA 起始检测论文：帧能量检测、背景类和检测后分类已经公开，但该研究使用 60 GHz FMCW，不是本项目 UWB 设备/数据的直接实验先例。UWB-Gestures原始描述明确每90个slow-time值即构成一个手势样本；当前恢复包没有包含手势前背景的连续雷达流，也没有逐帧起始真值。因而在固定90帧样本上做前缀分类不能度量在线起始延迟、背景误触发或流式分割。旧event-extent筛查的能量区间不是起始标注，且其固定裁剪门槛已失败，不改名重跑。

结论：通用雷达手势起始检测已非新主张；UWB专用版本或许可以另行研究，但现有数据不可验证。需要新的连续UWB背景+手势流、同步起始标注、参与者独立评估及可用采集接口。云端目前没有X4采集/标注设备，没有训练进程。本轮未读标签/雷达数组，无拟合或训练。整体目标未达成。来源、边界及检查记录见 `research/GESTURE_ONSET_FEASIBILITY_REVIEW_20261008.md`、`results/cloud_20261008_onset_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`。

## 2026-10-08 速度尺度多尺度直接先例全文复核

补充复核 Kim 等 2026 年 3 月 IEEE Access 论文《Robust UWB Radar Gesture Recognition Addressing Speed-Induced Scale Variations via Multi-Scale Feature Extraction》（DOI 10.1109/ACCESS.2026.3671243）完整八页出版版。该文使用同一 UWB-Gestures 数据、三台 X4 的 left/top/right 视角、range-Doppler 表示和并行 3×3/5×5/7×7 多尺度首层，以速度诱导的时频尺度变化为目标。因此“UWB手势速度鲁棒多尺度识别”不能作为本项目原创主张，也不复刻其模块后改称新方法。

论文报告98.12%，但本次检查的实验段落未说明参与者拆分和held-out用户ID；不据此推断泄漏，也不与本项目参与者留出的八折分数直接比较。本项目既有时间轴 dilation 筛选仍是封存的负门槛结果（相对range dilation仅+0.1301pp，3/6用户胜，未过预注册联合门槛），且没有逐样本真实手速真值。完整来源与技术边界见 `research/UWB_SPEED_SCALE_PRIOR_ART_REVIEW_20261008.md` 和 `results/cloud_20261008_speed_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`。本次仅文献复核，未读取标签/雷达数组，未拟合/训练；目标仍未达成。

- 外层测试用户 h=1..8，验证用户 h%8+1，其余六人训练。源拟合的平滑矩阵、PCA、方差等仅源六人拟合。
- Right 幅度/相位运动 concat 输入 [B,1,90,378]，复用旧 `cross_user_meta/trainer.py` warmup。Adam 1e-4，weight decay 1e-4；batch128/micro32；最少12、最多40 epochs，验证 CE 最早最小，patience8。上一轮模型种子 2026。
- A 的校准查询与 B 的完整查询不同；跨路线直接相减会混淆口径。旧报告另有 7440 条严格对齐查询。
- 只有8名研发用户，旧测试反馈已经多次使用。新机制选择是探索性开发；多种子不能替代独立新用户确认。

## 已完成但失败的两条新机制

| 方法/对照 | 历史结果 | 含义 |
|---|---:|---|
| 部分动作低秩 rank2 用户校准 | 未示范已知动作 macro accuracy 68.951373% | 同预算简单校准为72.751147%，差−3.799774pp，仅1/8用户改善；停止该机制 |
| 共享真实三路模型 LTR | 用户层 macro accuracy 93.441667% | 真实多雷达有效的候选工程基础，不等于新机制已成立 |
| 验证选择固定两路 | 89.536932% | 固定组合对照；与全局固定TR口径不同 |
| 主置信度级联 | 91.920833%，平均2.00461553路 | 严格超过原来≤2路门槛 |
| 学习收益选路 | 90.863258%，平均2.00787311路 | 原精度/成本/逐用户门槛未通过，停止该机制 |
| 收益选路处理中位延迟 | 26.3057ms；固定三路17.3396ms | 处理少一路没有带来该实现的加速 |

三部硬件都已安装，动作三路数据已同步采集。上述计时从已获得去杂波片段开始，包括请求视角的变换/编码/融合，排除采集、文件读取、静态杂波去除和加载。不能宣称全过程实时或采集功耗节省。

旧决策：`STOP_LOW_RANK_MECHANISM`、`STOP_VALUE_POLICY_MECHANISM`。oracle 使用标签，只作冻结分类器和指定动作族的机会诊断，不可部署。报告中特别澄清启动策略的机会判断曾读取测试标签，不能称其完全未经探索。

旧记录：145项测试通过、1项可选检查跳过，完整八折、校准支持重复、预算预测、策略与计时都有保存。**这些是上一轮的检查，不是本次云端测试成绩。**

## 2026-10-09 — Multi-view/dropout method gate

A full-text review of the 2026 MV-GLFNet paper shows same-dataset X4 UWB single/dual/tri-view and view-subset evaluation; the paper uses six train and two test subjects, unlike this project's eight one-user-held folds. Zhao et al. (VTC 2023) evaluates incomplete samples after one of two FMCW radars fails; Yang et al. (IET RSN 2018) studies degraded-view robustness with two angularly diverse CW radars; ModDrop already introduces random modality dropping for gesture recognition. Generic sensor-dropout, view-subset fusion, and angular-diversity claims are therefore closed as standalone novelties. No labels/arrays were read or training started. Report: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261009_multiview_dropout_prior_art/RUN_REPORT.md`.



## 2026-10-09 — same-dataset open-set prior art

A new bounded primary-source review closes generic open-set UWB gesture recognition as a standalone novelty on the current corpus. Eom et al. (2025, DOI 10.1109/JIOT.2025.3556901) publish UWB impulse-radar open-set recognition; Yang et al. (2026, DOI 10.1088/1361-6501/ae7fab) explicitly reports open-set results on the same UWB-Gestures corpus, five held-out class splits, and cross-subject analysis. A new threshold/inspector or class split is not enough. The paper split is not shown to match this project's eight user-held folds, so do not compare scores directly. # 2026-10-12 — empty-derived subspace classifier candidate remains uncleared

A source-only diagnostic found that an uncentered subspace learned from empty-class clips selectively attenuated empty examples in Left and Right views, but it did not measure recognition. I reviewed the prospective next step before fitting. US20200242342A1 (priority 2017-02-16) is explicitly an IR-UWB-capable radar gesture-recognition disclosure: subtract a no-gesture background signal, derive PCA eigenvectors from the recognizable gestures, project signals by inner products, and classify the coefficients. The specific empty-only nuisance-basis projection differs from the patent's gesture-derived basis; however, the broad constituent ideas are already disclosed. Existing IR-UWB low-rank clutter-subspace removal for moving-target detection adds adjacent signal-processing prior art. I therefore do not clear a standalone novelty claim for “empty reference + eigen/subspace projection + gesture classifier.” No legal assessment of patent scope/status is implied.

Decision: do not run the optional classifier/eight-fold study from the 2026-10-07 positive energy screen. That screen was eligibility only, not efficacy or novelty. A next method must make a technically material distinction supported by primary literature and be feasible with the actual clutter-removed cache; otherwise the needed input is a new independently annotated/calibrated acquisition rather than another model grid. Review and citations: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261012_empty_subspace_novelty_gate/RUN_REPORT.md`. # 2026-10-12 — Range-resolved envelope-spectrum prior-art gate

A bounded primary-source review screened per-range slow-time spectra computed from the cached clutter-removed maps. Ritchie & Jones (IEEE RadarConf 2019, DOI 10.1109/RADAR.2019.8835782; accepted manuscript read) already classify gestures from spectrograms per range bin. Their 24 GHz FMCW signal is not an exact UWB replication, but the generic range-resolved time-frequency representation is not cleared as a contribution. The UWB-Gestures descriptor specifies 20 frames/s and 90 rows per 4.5 s gesture (Ahmed et al., Scientific Data 2021, DOI 10.1038/s41597-021-00876-0), so the stored real-valued maps support at most an envelope-modulation spectrum below 10 Hz Nyquist, not coherent phase-derived micro-Doppler. Xue et al. 2026 further covers slow-time MTI and temporal modeling on the same corpus. This candidate is stopped before fitting; no values, labels, or checkpoints were read and no training started. Full scope and citations: `results/cloud_20261012_range_bin_envelope_doppler_gate/RUN_REPORT.md`.

## 2026-10-28 — direct same-corpus MV-GLF full-text prior-art gate

A full publisher HTML paper by Xue, Yang, and Zhu (Frontiers in Neuroscience 20, 2026, article 1864448; DOI 10.3389/fnins.2026.1864448) was available and reviewed. It evaluates the public 12-class, 8-subject UWB-Gestures corpus with three XeThru X4 left/top/right views; describes MTI TR maps of 90×189; and proposes early view fusion plus local ConvMixer/global CNN–Transformer branches and gated/bilinear fusion (MV-GLFNet). It reports 98.29% under a six-subject train/two-subject test split. This directly preempts generic backbone, global/local branch, adaptive/bilinear fusion, and view-count comparison claims on this corpus. Its protocol and 9,600-sample count are not matched to this project’s eight-fold leave-one-user-out / 9,300 quality-retained records; do not compare scores as matched or claim independent validation. The article does not eliminate every distinct single-view signal-mechanism possibility. No project data, labels, checkpoints, or predictions were read; no training/fitting/download/Drive access occurred. The method-contribution goal remains unresolved. Full bounded decision: `uwb_direction1/results/cloud_20261028_mv_glf_direct_same_corpus_prior_art/DECISION_NOTE.md`.

## 2026-10-28 — range-time echo Betti-curve Stage A preregistered

The source-only Stage A protocol is frozen at uwb_direction1/research/range_time_betti_gate/STAGE_A.json with runner uwb_direction1/tools/range_time_betti_stage_a.py. It tests normalized upper-level-set Betti curves from real-valued clutter-removed range-time maps, compares them against amplitude-quantile and fixed spatial-shuffle controls, reports Left/Top/Right independently, uses six development participants only, fixed Ridge alpha 1, and hard 900 CPU / 900 wall-second limits. The bounded prior-art review found radar persistent-homology target detection but not gesture classification, and a same-corpus 2026 paper on generic global/local multi-view fusion without topological descriptors. This is a feasibility gate only, not a novelty claim. Users 7–8 labels are out of scope; no training or data-value read has occurred yet. A GO result only permits designing a separate frozen validation; no method contribution is validated here.
